counter[(r[\"head\"], r[\"tail\"], r[\"type\"])] += 1\n    if not counter:\n        return 0.0\n    rates = [v / N for v in counter.values()]\n    return float(np.mean(rates))\n\n\ndef logprobs:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    return float(np.mean(logprobs))\n\n\ndef safe_auroc(scores, labels):\n    scores = np.asarray(scores, dtype=float)\n    labels = np.asarray(labels, float(\"nan\")\n    ranks = rankdata(scores)\n    u = ranks[labels == 1].sum() - n_pos * (n_pos + 1) / 2\n    return float(u / (n_pos * n_neg))\n\n\ndef safe_spearman(x, y):\n    x, y = np.asarray(x, dtype=float), np.asarray(y, dtype=float)\n    mask = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n    x, y = x[mask], y[mask]\n    if len(x) < 3:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    return float(spearmanr(x, y).statistic)\n\n\ndef safe_kendall(x, y):\n    x, y = np.asarray(x, dtype=float), np.asarray(y, dtype=float)\n    mask = np.isfinite(x) & np.isfinite(y)\n    x, y = x[mask], y[mask]\n    if len(x) < 3:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    return float(kendalltau(x, y).statistic)\n\n\ndef normalize_for_ece(signal_name, values):\n    v = np.asarray(values, dtype=float)\n    if signal_name in mask = np.isfinite(conf)\n    conf, corr = conf[mask], corr[mask]\n    if len(conf) == 0:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    bin_edges = np.linspace(0, 1, n_bins + 1)\n    ece = 0.0\n    total = np.array(f1_vals, dtype=float)\n        binary_correct = (f1_arr >= 1.0).astype(int)\n\n        split_results = {\"n\": len(split_instances), \"pct_perfect\": float(binary_correct.mean())}\n ## exp_017 LLaMA CoNLL-2003 NER N=8 — 5-signal 结果

| Signal | ρ (full) | ρ (cond) | AUROC (full) | AUROC (cond) |
|--------|----------|----------|--------------|--------------|
| **SJ** | **0.4314** | **0.4356** | 0.7039 | 0.7225 |
| FK | 0.4277 | 0.4310 | 0.7026 | 0.7208 |
| logprob | 0.3072 | 0.3282 | **0.7610** | **0.8102** |
| EM | 0.4306 | **0.4403** | 0.7046 | 0.7257 |
| voting_conf | 0.4256 | 0.3743 | 0.7088 | 0.6989 |

**Conclusion: success**
- SJ ρ=0.431 > logprob ρ=0.307（Δ=+0.124），结构信号在相关性维度胜出
- ρ-AUROC 分离持续存在：logprob AUROC=0.761 > SJ AUROC=0.704
- EM 在 conditional split 上 ρ 最高（0.4403）
- N=8（非 N=16），因 3453 test instances 成本约束
- 与 exp_002（Qwen CoNLL N=8）和 exp_007（LLaMA SciERC N=8）直接可比
